ب:B004/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Poverty Changed into Sufficiency","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_67451475a37eb65bd5c8","text":"Vulnerability is encountered and answered through shelter, nurture, provision, and responsive giving rather than neglect or rebuke.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Deposit and Safekeeping","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_67ca0179424e9fae203c","text":"Property is handed over, held under another's care, maintained, and returned at a later time.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Layered Cloud, Soft Wind, and Green Persistence","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6ba8647c88d7c6ea25c4","text":"Layering gives the cloud mass, lingering gives it duration, and the soft wind gives the weather motion without violence. The green plant is the durable terrestrial outcome of that sustained atmosphere.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Poverty Changed into Sufficiency","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6e8cdd9433db507de308","text":"93:3, 93:5, 93:11 `رَبُّكَ` and `رَبِّكَ`; 93:4 `ٱلْأُولَىٰ`; 93:6-8 `يَجِدْكَ` and `وَجَدَكَ`; 93:8 `عَآئِلًا`, `فَأَغْنَىٰ`; 93:11 `بِنِعْمَةِ`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Foster Parent and Ward","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_6fff9320223fb8750abf","text":"A young dependent from an earlier household is received by a foster parent or nurse and raised as a ward.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Telling the Benefaction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_73cf43ce0300af0110dc","text":"Benefaction supplies the content, the giver supplies its relational source, narration turns it into speech, and manifestation lets the spoken good become publicly perceptible.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Household, Dependents, and Service","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_766dacf6d29c8bdd32c8","text":"The household supplies the social container, dependents supply those maintained within it, and service supplies the repeated transfer of labor and necessities. The multitude motif scales the same relation from one family to a gathered group.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"context_only","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"A:Household, Dependents, and Service","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_7678981012670bf33af4","text":"People, property, and dependents are gathered into durable relations of maintenance, service, guardianship, and entrusted keeping.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000019/B002","root_000067/B004","root_000532/B002","root_001635/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"branch_nomination","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"E:Deposit and Safekeeping","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_76b268c28126fd1392a6","text":"entrusted deposit `و د ع:B005/m01`; custodial oversight and repair `ر ب ب:B002/m01`; ordered estate keeping `ء و ل:B004/m02`; deferred transfer `ء خ ر:B002/m01`","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"B:Telling the Benefaction","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_77fb5e8c6d4b4704f8d2","text":"A received good is made visible by being recounted as news.","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"macro","source_local_id":"C:Obedience, Attachment, and Surety","source_type":"channel","support_id":"sup_782fddcff7048d036600","text":"The principal supplies the relation's authoritative pole.
